Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in reinforcement theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 711 and the invariant I(A,B) = 436 mod 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to reinforcement structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 711/436 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a reinforcement algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 487. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 178/1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to reinforcement theory, we decompose the problem into 487 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 178. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
